Original range: 613-632
Documentation:

Method code:
613 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
614 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
615 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
616 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
617 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
618 |       if (typeSet == null) {
619 |         typeSet = Maps.newHashMap();
620 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
621 |       }
622 |       ObjectType objType = (ObjectType) type;
623 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
624 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {
625 |       addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
626 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
627 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
628 |       for (JSType alternate : ((UnionType) type).getAlternates()) {
629 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);
630 |       }
631 |     }
632 |   }

===== M0036 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-150
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
128 |               current.isUnknownType()) {
129 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
130 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
131 |               return this;
132 |             }
133 |           } else {
134 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
135 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
136 |               return this;
137 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
138 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
139 |               it.remove();
140 |             }
141 |           }
142 |         }
143 |         alternates.add(alternate);
144 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
145 |       }
146 |     } else {
147 |       result = null;
148 |     }
149 |     return this;
150 |   }

===== M0037 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 653-679
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
653 |   @Override
654 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
655 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
656 |       return false;
657 |     }
658 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
659 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
660 |       return false;
661 |     }
662 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
663 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
664 |         return this == that;
665 |       }
666 |       return false;
667 |     }
668 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
669 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
670 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
671 |       }
672 |       return false;
673 |     }
674 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
675 |       return false;
676 |     }
677 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
678 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
679 |   }

===== M0038 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isStatement(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1333-1347
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the node is used as a statement.
   */
Method code:
1333 |   static boolean isStatement(Node n) {
1334 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1335 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1336 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1337 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.
1338 |     Preconditions.checkState(parent != null);
1339 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1340 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1341 |       case Token.BLOCK:
1342 |       case Token.LABEL:
1343 |         return true;
1344 |       default:
1345 |         return false;
1346 |     }
1347 |   }

===== M0041 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.visitPreOrder(Node,Visitor,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2288-2298
Documentation:
/**
   * A pre-order traversal, calling Vistor.visit for each child matching
   * the predicate.
   */
Method code:
2288 |   static void visitPreOrder(Node node,
2289 |                      Visitor vistor,
2290 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2291 |     vistor.visit(node);
2292 | 
2293 |     if (traverseChildrenPred.apply(node)) {
2294 |       for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2295 |         visitPreOrder(c, vistor, traverseChildrenPred);
2296 |       }
2297 |     }
2298 |   }

===== M0051 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1152 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1153 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1154 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1155 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1156 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1157 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1158 |       } else {
1159 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1160 |       }
1161 |     }
1162 |     return builder.build();
1163 |   }

===== M0053 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 530-673
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
530 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
531 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
532 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
533 |     // that we know to be safe
534 |     switch (n.getType()) {
535 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
536 |       case Token.AND:
537 |       case Token.BLOCK:
538 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
539 |       case Token.HOOK:
540 |       case Token.IF:
541 |       case Token.IN:
542 |       case Token.LP:
543 |       case Token.NUMBER:
544 |       case Token.OR:
545 |       case Token.THIS:
546 |       case Token.TRUE:
547 |       case Token.FALSE:
548 |       case Token.NULL:
549 |       case Token.STRING:
550 |       case Token.SWITCH:
551 |       case Token.TRY:
552 |       case Token.EMPTY:
553 |         break;
554 | 
555 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
556 |       case Token.THROW:
557 |         return true;
558 | 
559 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
560 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
561 |           return true;
562 |         }
563 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
564 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
565 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
566 |             return true;
567 |           }
568 |         }
569 |         return false;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
572 |       case Token.REGEXP:
573 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
574 |           return true;
575 |         }
576 |         break;
577 | 
578 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
579 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
580 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
581 |           return true;
582 |         }
583 |         break;
584 | 
585 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
586 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
587 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
588 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
589 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
590 | 
591 |       case Token.NEW:
592 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
593 |           return true;
594 |         }
595 | 
596 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
597 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
598 |           // side-effects
599 |           break;
600 |         }
601 |         return true;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.CALL:
604 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
605 |         // side effect property set.
606 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
607 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
608 |           // side-effects
609 |           break;
610 |         }
611 |         return true;
612 | 
613 |       default:
614 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType())) {
615 |           break;
616 |         }
617 | 
618 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
619 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
620 |           if (isName(assignTarget)) {
621 |             return true;
622 |           }
623 | 
624 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
625 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
626 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
627 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
628 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
629 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
630 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
631 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
632 |             return true;
633 |           }
634 | 
635 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
636 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
637 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
638 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
639 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
640 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
641 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
642 |               return false;
643 |             }
644 | 
645 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
646 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
647 |             // other objects.
648 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
649 |             // side-effect.
650 |             while (isGet(current)) {
651 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
652 |             }
653 | 
654 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
655 |           } else {
656 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
657 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
658 |             // preserve existing behavior.
659 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
660 |           }
661 |         }
662 | 
663 |         return true;
664 |     }
665 | 
666 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
667 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
668 |         return true;
669 |       }
670 |     }
671 | 
672 |     return false;
673 |   }

===== M0056 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1019-1039
Documentation:

Method code:
1019 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1020 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1021 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1022 |       return s;
1023 |     }
1024 | 
1025 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1026 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1027 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1028 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1029 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1030 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1031 |       // crazy.
1032 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1033 |         sb.append(c);
1034 |       } else {
1035 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1036 |       }
1037 |     }
1038 |     return sb.toString();
1039 |   }

===== M0059 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.UnreachableCodeElimination.removeDeadExprStatementSafely(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/UnreachableCodeElimination.java
Original range: 199-235
Documentation:

Method code:
199 |   private void removeDeadExprStatementSafely(Node n) {
200 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
201 |     if (n.getType() == Token.EMPTY ||
202 |         (n.getType() == Token.BLOCK && !n.hasChildren())) {
203 |       // Not always trivial to remove, let FoldContants work its magic later.
204 |       return;
205 |     }
206 | 
207 |     switch (n.getType()) {
208 |       // Removing an unreachable DO node is messy because it means we still have
209 |       // to execute one iteration. If the DO's body has breaks in the middle, it
210 |       // can get even more trickier and code size might actually increase.
211 |       case Token.DO:
212 |         return;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.BLOCK:
215 |         // BLOCKs are used in several ways including wrapping CATCH blocks in TRYs
216 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.TRY) {
217 |           if (NodeUtil.isTryCatchNodeContainer(n)) {
218 |             return;
219 |           }
220 |         }
221 |         break;
222 | 
223 |       case Token.CATCH:
224 |         Node tryNode = parent.getParent();
225 |         NodeUtil.maybeAddFinally(tryNode);
226 |         break;
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     NodeUtil.redeclareVarsInsideBranch(n);
230 |     compiler.reportCodeChange();
231 |     if (logger.isLoggable(Level.FINE)) {
232 |       logger.fine("Removing " + n.toString());
233 |     }
234 |     NodeUtil.removeChild(n.getParent(), n);
235 |   }

596 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,

===== M0076 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformTokenType(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 872-1183
Documentation:

Method code:
872 |   private static int transformTokenType(int token) {
873 |     switch (token) {
874 |       case com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Token.ERROR:
875 |         return Token.ERROR;
876 |       case com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Token.EOF:
877 |         return Token.EOF;
878 |       case com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Token.EOL:
879 |         return Token.EOL;
880 |       case com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Token.ENTERWITH:
881 |         return Token.ENTERWITH;
882 |       case com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Token.LEAVEWITH:
883 |         return Token.LEAVEWITH;
884 |       case com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Token.RETURN:

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
85 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
86 |                            String sourceString,
87 |                            Config config,
88 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
89 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
90 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
91 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
92 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
93 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
94 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
95 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
96 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
97 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
98 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
99 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
100 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
104 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
105 |     try {
106 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
107 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
108 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
109 |     } finally {
110 |       Context.exit();
111 |     }
112 |     Node root = null;
113 |     if (astRoot != null) {
114 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
115 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
116 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
117 |     }
118 |     return root;
119 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
250 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
251 |     this.type = type;
252 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
253 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
254 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
255 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
256 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
257 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
258 |       int i = 0;
259 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
260 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
261 |         i++;
262 |       }
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
266 | 
267 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
268 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
269 |       return;
270 |     }
271 | 
272 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
273 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
274 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
275 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
276 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
277 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
278 |       trace.truncateEvents();
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
282 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
283 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
284 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
285 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
286 |                     + "try/finally block. "
287 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
288 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
289 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     trace.startEvent(this);
293 |   }

262 |   private int position2charno(int position) {
263 |     int lineIndex = sourceString.lastIndexOf('\n', position);
264 |     if (lineIndex == -1) {
265 |       return position;

===== M0089 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 422-466
Documentation:

Method code:
422 |   @Override
423 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
424 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
425 |       return true;
426 |     }
427 | 
428 |     // Union types
429 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
430 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
431 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
432 |       return false;
433 |     }
434 | 
435 |     // record types
436 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
437 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Interfaces
441 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
442 |     // to the interface instance.
443 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
444 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
445 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
446 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
447 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
448 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
449 |           return true;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // other prototype based objects
455 |     if (that != null) {
456 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
457 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
458 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
459 |         // to avoid guessing.
460 |         return true;
461 |       }
462 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
463 |     }
464 | 
465 |     return false;
466 |   }

===== M0091 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 675-772
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
675 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
676 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
677 |     /*
678 |      * This is the case where:
679 |      *
680 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
681 |      * the script.
682 |      *
683 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
684 |      * the caller of the function.
685 |      *
686 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
687 |      * back to the caller of the function.
688 |      *
689 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
690 |      * asked to traverse.
691 |      *
692 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
693 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
694 |      */
695 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
696 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
697 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
698 |       return null;
699 |     }
700 | 
701 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
702 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
703 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
704 |       case Token.IF:
705 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
706 |       case Token.CASE:
707 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
708 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
709 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
710 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
711 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
712 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
713 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
714 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
715 |           } else {
716 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
717 |           }
718 |         } else {
719 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
720 |         }
721 |         break;
722 |       case Token.FOR:
723 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
724 |           return parent;
725 |         } else {
726 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
727 |         }
728 |       case Token.WHILE:
729 |       case Token.DO:
730 |         return parent;
731 |       case Token.TRY:
732 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
733 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
734 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
735 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
736 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
737 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
738 |           }
739 |         // CATCH block.
740 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
741 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
742 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
743 |           } else {
744 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
745 |           }
746 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
747 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
748 |           if (cfa != null) {
749 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
750 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
751 |             }
752 |           }
753 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
754 |         }
755 |     }
756 | 
757 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
758 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
759 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
760 | 
761 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
762 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
763 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
764 |     }
765 | 
766 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
767 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
768 |     } else {
769 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
770 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |     }
772 |   }

===== M0092 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 117-170
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
117 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
118 |     switch (n.getType()) {
119 |       case Token.VAR:
120 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
121 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
122 |              child != null;) {
123 |           Node next = child.getNext();
124 |           declareVar(child);
125 |           child = next;
126 |         }
127 |         return;
128 | 
129 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
130 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
131 |           return;
132 |         }
133 | 
134 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
135 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
136 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
137 |           return;
138 |         }
139 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
140 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
141 | 
142 |       case Token.CATCH:
143 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
144 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
145 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
146 |         // is the code block
147 | 
148 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
149 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
150 | 
151 |         declareVar(var);
152 |         scanVars(block, n);
153 |         return;  // only one child to scan
154 | 
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
157 |         break;
158 |     }
159 | 
160 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
161 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
162 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
163 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
164 |            child != null;) {
165 |         Node next = child.getNext();
166 |         scanVars(child, n);
167 |         child = next;
168 |       }
169 |     }
170 |   }

===== M0096 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 813-840
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
813 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
814 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
815 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
816 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
817 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
818 |           return;
819 |         }
820 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
821 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
822 | 
823 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
824 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
825 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
826 |           } else {
827 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
828 |           }
829 |         } else { // Has a catch.
830 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
831 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
832 |             return;
833 |           } else {
834 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
835 |           }
836 |         }
837 |         lastJump = handler;
838 |       }
839 |     }
840 |   }

55 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
56 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {

58 |       addGuards(Lists.reverse(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards));

Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
150 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
151 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
152 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
153 |       case Token.BLOCK:
154 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
155 |       case Token.TRY:
156 |       case Token.FINALLY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0122 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1595-1632
Documentation:
/** Returns true if this node is equivalent semantically to another */
Method code:
1595 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node) {
1596 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1597 |       try {
1598 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1599 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1600 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1601 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1602 |             return false;
1603 |           }
1604 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1605 |           return false;
1606 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1607 |           return false;
1608 |         } else {
1609 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1610 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1611 |               return false;
1612 |             }
1613 |           }
1614 |         }
1615 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1616 |         return false;
1617 |       }
1618 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1619 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1620 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1621 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1622 |         return false;
1623 |       }
1624 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1625 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1626 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1627 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1628 |         return false;
1629 |       }
1630 |     }
1631 |     return true;
1632 |   }

===== M0137 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.removeChild(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1381-1434
Documentation:
/** Safely remove children while maintaining a valid node structure. */
Method code:
1381 |   static void removeChild(Node parent, Node node) {
1382 |     if (isTryFinallyNode(parent, node)) {
1383 |       if (NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(getCatchBlock(parent))) {
1384 |         // A finally can only be removed if there is a catch.
1385 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1386 |       } else {
1387 |         // Otherwise only its children can be removed.
1388 |         node.detachChildren();
1389 |       }
1390 |     } else if (node.getType() == Token.CATCH) {
1391 |       // The CATCH can can only be removed if there is a finally clause.
1392 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent().getParent();
1393 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1394 |       node.detachFromParent();
1395 |     } else if (isTryCatchNodeContainer(node)) {
1396 |       // The container node itself can't be removed, but the contained CATCH
1397 |       // can if there is a 'finally' clause
1398 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent();
1399 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1400 |       node.detachChildren();
1401 |     } else if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
1402 |       // Simply empty the block.  This maintains source location and
1403 |       // "synthetic"-ness.
1404 |       node.detachChildren();
1405 |     } else if (isStatementBlock(parent)
1406 |         || isSwitchCase(node)) {
1407 |       // A statement in a block can simply be removed.
1408 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1409 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
1410 |       if (parent.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1411 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1412 |       } else {
1413 |         // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1414 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1415 |         // This would leave an empty VAR, remove the VAR itself.
1416 |         removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1417 |       }
1418 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.LABEL
1419 |         && node == parent.getLastChild()) {
1420 |       // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1421 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1422 |       // A LABEL without children can not be referred to, remove it.
1423 |       removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1424 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.FOR
1425 |         && parent.getChildCount() == 4) {
1426 |       // Only Token.FOR can have an Token.EMPTY other control structure
1427 |       // need something for the condition. Others need to be replaced
1428 |       // or the structure removed.
1429 |       parent.replaceChild(node, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1430 |     } else {
1431 |       throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid attempt to remove node: " +
1432 |           node.toString() + " of "+ parent.toString());
1433 |     }
1434 |   }

===== M0142 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

===== M0143 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2007-2017
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2007 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2008 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2009 |     int len = s.length();
2010 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2011 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2012 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2013 |         return false;
2014 |       }
2015 |     }
2016 |     return true;
2017 |   }

===== M0150 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.FixedPointGraphTraversal.computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E>,Set<N>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/FixedPointGraphTraversal.java
Original range: 93-128
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute a fixed point for the given graph, entering from the given nodes.
   * @param graph The graph to traverse.
   * @param entrySet The nodes to begin traversing from.
   */
Method code:
93 |   public void computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E> graph, Set<N> entrySet) {
94 |     int cycleCount = 0;
95 |     long nodeCount = graph.getNodes().size();
96 | 
97 |     // Choose a bail-out heuristically in case the computation
98 |     // doesn't converge.
99 |     long maxIterations = Math.max(nodeCount * nodeCount * nodeCount, 100);
100 | 
101 |     // Use a LinkedHashSet, so that the traversal is deterministic.
102 |     LinkedHashSet<DiGraphNode<N, E>> workSet =
103 |         Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
104 |     for (N n : entrySet) {
105 |       workSet.add(graph.getDirectedGraphNode(n));
106 |     }
107 |     for (; !workSet.isEmpty() && cycleCount < maxIterations; cycleCount++) {
108 |       // For every out edge in the workSet, traverse that edge. If that
109 |       // edge updates the state of the graph, then add the destination
110 |       // node to the resultSet, so that we can update all of its out edges
111 |       // on the next iteration.
112 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> source = workSet.iterator().next();
113 |       N sourceValue = source.getValue();
114 | 
115 |       workSet.remove(source);
116 | 
117 |       List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> outEdges = source.getOutEdges();
118 |       for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> edge : outEdges) {
119 |         N destNode = edge.getDestination().getValue();
120 |         if (callback.traverseEdge(sourceValue, edge.getValue(), destNode)) {
121 |           workSet.add(edge.getDestination());
122 |         }
123 |       }
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     Preconditions.checkState(cycleCount != maxIterations,
127 |         NON_HALTING_ERROR_MSG);
128 |   }

===== M0154 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

/**

171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);

176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +

180 |         }

182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {

231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);

254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.VAR:
265 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {

274 |         }
275 |       case Token.WHILE:

277 |       case Token.WITH:

279 |     }

===== M0181 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 204-228
Documentation:

Method code:
204 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
205 |     JSDocInfo jsDocInfo = handleJsDoc(node);
206 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
207 |     if (jsDocInfo != null) {
208 |       irNode.setJSDocInfo(jsDocInfo);
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
212 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
213 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
214 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
215 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
216 |     } else {
217 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
218 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
219 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
220 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
221 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
222 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
223 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
224 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
225 |       }
226 |     }
227 |     return irNode;
228 |   }

===== M0200 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 295-303
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
295 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
296 |       Node ret = transform(n);
297 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
298 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
299 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
300 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
301 |       }
302 |       return ret;
303 |     }

===== M0217 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 923-1039
Documentation:

Method code:
923 |   private void toString(
924 |       StringBuilder sb,
925 |       boolean printSource,
926 |       boolean printAnnotations,
927 |       boolean printType) {
928 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
929 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
930 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
931 |         sb.append(' ');
932 |         sb.append(getString());
933 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
934 |         sb.append(' ');
935 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
936 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
937 |         // toStringTree.
938 |         if (first.getType() == Token.STRING) {
939 |           sb.append(first.getString());
940 |         }
941 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
942 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
943 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
944 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
945 |           sb.append(' ');
946 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
947 |         }
948 |         if (printSource) {
949 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
950 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
951 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
952 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
953 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
954 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
955 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
956 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
957 |           sb.append(']');
958 |         }
959 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
960 |         sb.append(' ');
961 |         sb.append(getDouble());
962 |       }
963 |       if (printSource) {
964 |         int lineno = getLineno();
965 |         if (lineno != -1) {
966 |           sb.append(' ');
967 |           sb.append(lineno);
968 |         }
969 |       }
970 | 
971 |       if (printAnnotations) {
972 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
973 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
974 |           int type = keys[i];
975 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
976 |           sb.append(" [");
977 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
978 |           sb.append(": ");
979 |           String value;
980 |           switch (type) {
981 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
982 |               value = "target block property";
983 |               break;
984 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
985 |               value = "last local block";
986 |               break;
987 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
988 |               switch (x.intValue) {
989 |                 case BOTH:
990 |                   value = "both";
991 |                   break;
992 |                 case RIGHT:
993 |                   value = "right";
994 |                   break;
995 |                 case LEFT:
996 |                   value = "left";
997 |                   break;
998 |                 default:
999 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1000 |               }
1001 |               break;
1002 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1003 |               switch (x.intValue) {
1004 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1005 |                   value = "eval";
1006 |                   break;
1007 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1008 |                   value = "with";
1009 |                   break;
1010 |                 default:
1011 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1012 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1013 |               }
1014 |               break;
1015 |             default:
1016 |               Object obj = x.objectValue;
1017 |               if (obj != null) {
1018 |                 value = obj.toString();
1019 |               } else {
1020 |                 value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
1021 |               }
1022 |               break;
1023 |           }
1024 |           sb.append(value);
1025 |           sb.append(']');
1026 |         }
1027 |       }
1028 | 
1029 |       if (printType) {
1030 |         if (jsType != null) {
1031 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1032 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1033 |             sb.append(" : ");
1034 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1035 |           }
1036 |         }
1037 |       }
1038 |     }
1039 |   }

===== M0221 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 395-411
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
395 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
396 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
397 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
398 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
399 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
400 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
401 |         String inputName = input.getName();
402 | 
403 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
404 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
405 |         // will get an error down the line.
406 |         inputs.add(input);
407 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
408 |       }
409 |     }
410 |     return inputs;
411 |   }
