===== M0225 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 75-712
Documentation:

Method code:
75 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
76 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
77 |       return;
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     int type = n.getType();
81 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
82 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
83 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
84 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
85 | 
86 |     // Handle all binary operators
87 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
88 |       Preconditions.checkState(
89 |           childCount == 2,
90 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
91 |           opstr, childCount);
92 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
93 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
94 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
95 | 
96 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
97 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
98 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
99 | 
100 |       // Handle associativity.
101 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
102 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
103 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
104 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
105 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
106 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
107 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
108 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
109 |       } else {
110 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
111 |       }
112 |       return;
113 |     }
114 | 
115 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
116 | 
117 |     switch (type) {
118 |       case Token.TRY: {
119 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
120 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
121 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
122 | 
123 |         add("try");
124 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
125 | 
126 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
127 |         // isn't a catch block
128 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
129 |         if (catchblock != null) {
130 |           add(catchblock);
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         if (childCount == 3) {
134 |           add("finally");
135 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
136 |         }
137 |         break;
138 |       }
139 | 
140 |       case Token.CATCH:
141 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
142 |         add("catch(");
143 |         add(first);
144 |         add(")");
145 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
146 |         break;
147 | 
148 |       case Token.THROW:
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
150 |         add("throw");
151 |         add(first);
152 | 
153 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
154 |         // parse this.
155 |         cc.endStatement(true);
156 |         break;
157 | 
158 |       case Token.RETURN:
159 |         add("return");
160 |         if (childCount == 1) {
161 |           add(first);
162 |         } else {
163 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
164 |         }
165 |         cc.endStatement();
166 |         break;
167 | 
168 |       case Token.VAR:
169 |         if (first != null) {
170 |           add("var ");
171 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
172 |         }
173 |         break;
174 | 
175 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
176 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
177 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.NAME:
181 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
182 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
183 |         } else {
184 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
185 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
186 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
187 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
188 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
189 |           } else {
190 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
191 |             // precedence.
192 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
193 |           }
194 |         }
195 |         break;
196 | 
197 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
198 |         add("[");
199 |         addList(first, (int[]) n.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP));
200 |         add("]");
201 |         break;
202 | 
203 |       case Token.LP:
204 |         add("(");
205 |         addList(first);
206 |         add(")");
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.COMMA:
210 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
211 |         addList(first, false, context);
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.NUMBER:
215 |         Preconditions.checkState(
216 |             childCount ==
217 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
218 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0));
219 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
220 |         break;
221 | 
222 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
223 |       case Token.VOID:
224 |       case Token.NOT:
225 |       case Token.BITNOT:
226 |       case Token.POS: {
227 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
228 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
229 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
230 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
231 |         break;
232 |       }
233 | 
234 |       case Token.NEG: {
235 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
236 | 
237 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
238 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
239 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
240 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
241 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
242 |         } else {
243 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
244 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
245 |         }
246 | 
247 |         break;
248 |       }
249 | 
250 |       case Token.HOOK: {
251 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
252 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
253 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
254 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
255 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
256 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
257 |         addExpr(last, 1);
258 |         break;
259 |       }
260 | 
261 |       case Token.REGEXP:
262 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
263 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
264 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
265 |         }
266 | 
267 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
268 | 
269 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
270 |         if (childCount == 2) {
271 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
272 |         } else {
273 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
274 |           add(regexp);
275 |         }
276 |         break;
277 | 
278 |       case Token.GET_REF:
279 |         add(first);
280 |         break;
281 | 
282 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
283 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
284 |         add(first);
285 |         add(".");
286 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
287 |         break;
288 | 
289 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
290 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
291 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
292 |         }
293 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
294 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
295 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
296 |           add("(");
297 |         }
298 | 
299 |         add("function");
300 |         add(first);
301 | 
302 |         add(first.getNext());
303 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
304 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
305 | 
306 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
307 |           add(")");
308 |         }
309 |         break;
310 | 
311 |       case Token.GET:
312 |       case Token.SET:
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
314 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
315 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
316 | 
317 |         // Get methods are unnamed
318 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
319 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
320 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
321 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
322 |           add("get ");
323 |         } else {
324 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
325 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
326 |           add("set ");
327 |         }
328 | 
329 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
330 |         String name = n.getString();
331 |         Node fn = first;
332 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
333 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
334 | 
335 |         // Add the property name.
336 |         if (TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
337 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
338 |             // unicode escaped.
339 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
340 |           add(name);
341 |         } else {
342 |           add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
343 |         }
344 | 
345 |         add(parameters);
346 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
347 |         break;
348 | 
349 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
350 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
351 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
352 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
353 |         }
354 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
355 |         if (preserveBlock) {
356 |           cc.beginBlock();
357 |         }
358 | 
359 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
360 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
361 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
362 |                 !preserveBlock &&
363 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
364 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
365 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
366 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
367 | 
368 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
369 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
370 |             cc.endStatement();
371 |           }
372 | 
373 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
374 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
375 |           }
376 | 
377 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
378 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
379 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
380 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
381 |           }
382 |         }
383 |         if (preserveBlock) {
384 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
385 |         }
386 |         break;
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       case Token.FOR:
390 |         if (childCount == 4) {
391 |           add("for(");
392 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
393 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
394 |           } else {
395 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
396 |           }
397 |           add(";");
398 |           add(first.getNext());
399 |           add(";");
400 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
401 |           add(")");
402 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
403 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
404 |         } else {
405 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
406 |           add("for(");
407 |           add(first);
408 |           add("in");
409 |           add(first.getNext());
410 |           add(")");
411 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
412 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
413 |         }
414 |         break;
415 | 
416 |       case Token.DO:
417 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
418 |         add("do");
419 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
420 |         add("while(");
421 |         add(last);
422 |         add(")");
423 |         cc.endStatement();
424 |         break;
425 | 
426 |       case Token.WHILE:
427 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
428 |         add("while(");
429 |         add(first);
430 |         add(")");
431 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
432 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
433 |         break;
434 | 
435 |       case Token.EMPTY:
436 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
437 |         break;
438 | 
439 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
440 |         Preconditions.checkState(
441 |             childCount == 2,
442 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
443 |         Preconditions.checkState(
444 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
445 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
446 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
447 |         if (needsParens) {
448 |           add("(");
449 |         }
450 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
451 |         if (needsParens) {
452 |           add(")");
453 |         }
454 |         add(".");
455 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
456 |         break;
457 |       }
458 | 
459 |       case Token.GETELEM:
460 |         Preconditions.checkState(
461 |             childCount == 2,
462 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
463 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
464 |         add("[");
465 |         add(first.getNext());
466 |         add("]");
467 |         break;
468 | 
469 |       case Token.WITH:
470 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
471 |         add("with(");
472 |         add(first);
473 |         add(")");
474 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
475 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
476 |         break;
477 | 
478 |       case Token.INC:
479 |       case Token.DEC: {
480 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
481 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
482 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
483 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
484 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
485 |         if (postProp != 0) {
486 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
487 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
488 |         } else {
489 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
490 |           add(first);
491 |         }
492 |         break;
493 |       }
494 | 
495 |       case Token.CALL:
496 |         // We have two special cases here:
497 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
498 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
499 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
500 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
501 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
502 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
503 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
504 |         // that must be preserved.
505 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
506 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
507 |           add("(0,");
508 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
509 |           add(")");
510 |         } else {
511 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
512 |         }
513 |         add("(");
514 |         addList(first.getNext());
515 |         add(")");
516 |         break;
517 | 
518 |       case Token.IF:
519 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
520 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
521 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
522 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
523 |           cc.beginBlock();
524 |         }
525 | 
526 |         add("if(");
527 |         add(first);
528 |         add(")");
529 | 
530 |         if (hasElse) {
531 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
532 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
533 |           add("else");
534 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
535 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
536 |         } else {
537 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
538 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
539 |         }
540 | 
541 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
542 |           cc.endBlock();
543 |         }
544 |         break;
545 | 
546 |       case Token.NULL:
547 |       case Token.THIS:
548 |       case Token.FALSE:
549 |       case Token.TRUE:
550 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
551 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
552 |         break;
553 | 
554 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
555 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
556 |         add("continue");
557 |         if (childCount == 1) {
558 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
559 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
560 |           }
561 |           add(" ");
562 |           add(first);
563 |         }
564 |         cc.endStatement();
565 |         break;
566 | 
567 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
568 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
569 |         add("debugger");
570 |         cc.endStatement();
571 |         break;
572 | 
573 |       case Token.BREAK:
574 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
575 |         add("break");
576 |         if (childCount == 1) {
577 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
578 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
579 |           }
580 |           add(" ");
581 |           add(first);
582 |         }
583 |         cc.endStatement();
584 |         break;
585 | 
586 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
587 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
588 | 
589 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
590 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
591 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
592 |         cc.endStatement();
593 |         break;
594 | 
595 |       case Token.NEW:
596 |         add("new ");
597 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
598 | 
599 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
600 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
601 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
602 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
603 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
604 |         }
605 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
606 | 
607 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
608 |         Node next = first.getNext();
609 |         if (next != null) {
610 |           add("(");
611 |           addList(next);
612 |           add(")");
613 |         }
614 |         break;
615 | 
616 |       case Token.STRING:
617 |         if (childCount !=
618 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
619 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
620 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
621 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
622 |         }
623 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
624 |         break;
625 | 
626 |       case Token.DELPROP:
627 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
628 |         add("delete ");
629 |         add(first);
630 |         break;
631 | 
632 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
633 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
634 |         if (needsParens) {
635 |           add("(");
636 |         }
637 |         add("{");
638 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
639 |           if (c != first) {
640 |             cc.listSeparator();
641 |           }
642 | 
643 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
644 |             add(c);
645 |           } else {
646 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
647 |             // not JavaScript keywords
648 |             if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&
649 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(c.getString()) &&
650 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(c.getString()) &&
651 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
652 |                 // unicode escaped.
653 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(c.getString())) {
654 |               add(c.getString());
655 |             } else {
656 |               addExpr(c, 1);
657 |             }
658 |             add(":");
659 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
660 |           }
661 |         }
662 |         add("}");
663 |         if (needsParens) {
664 |           add(")");
665 |         }
666 |         break;
667 |       }
668 | 
669 |       case Token.SWITCH:
670 |         add("switch(");
671 |         add(first);
672 |         add(")");
673 |         cc.beginBlock();
674 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
675 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
676 |         break;
677 | 
678 |       case Token.CASE:
679 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
680 |         add("case ");
681 |         add(first);
682 |         addCaseBody(last);
683 |         break;
684 | 
685 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
686 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
687 |         add("default");
688 |         addCaseBody(first);
689 |         break;
690 | 
691 |       case Token.LABEL:
692 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
693 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
694 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
695 |         }
696 |         add(first);
697 |         add(":");
698 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
699 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
700 |         break;
701 | 
702 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
703 |       // ignored for our purposes.
704 |       case Token.SETNAME:
705 |         break;
706 | 
707 |       default:
708 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
709 |     }
710 | 
711 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
712 |   }

===== M0231 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1557-1559
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1557 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1558 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1559 |   }

===== M0239 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0247 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 842-869
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
842 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
843 |     // unknown
844 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
845 |       return true;
846 |     }
847 |     // equality
848 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
849 |       return true;
850 |     }
851 |     // all type
852 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
853 |       return true;
854 |     }
855 |     // unions
856 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
857 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
858 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
859 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
860 |           return true;
861 |         }
862 |       }
863 |     }
864 |     // named types
865 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
866 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
867 |     }
868 |     return false;
869 |   }

===== M0248 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 907-934
Documentation:
/** Outputs a js string, using the optimal (single/double) quote character */
Method code:
907 |   static String jsString(String s, CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
908 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
909 | 
910 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
911 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
912 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
913 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
914 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
915 |       }
916 |     }
917 | 
918 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
919 |     char quote;
920 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
921 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
922 |       quote = '\'';
923 |       doublequote = "\"";
924 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
925 |     } else {
926 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
927 |       quote = '\"';
928 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
929 |       singlequote = "\'";
930 |     }
931 | 
932 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
933 |         outputCharsetEncoder);
934 |   }

===== M0257 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 956-1017
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
956 |   static String strEscape(String s, char quote,
957 |                           String doublequoteEscape,
958 |                           String singlequoteEscape,
959 |                           String backslashEscape,
960 |                           CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
961 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
962 |     sb.append(quote);
963 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
964 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
965 |       switch (c) {
966 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
967 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
968 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
969 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
970 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
971 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
972 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
973 |           if (i >= 2 &&
974 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
975 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
976 |             sb.append("\\>");
977 |           } else {
978 |             sb.append(c);
979 |           }
980 |           break;
981 |         case '<':                       // Break </script into <\/script
982 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
983 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
984 |               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
985 |             sb.append("<\\");
986 |           } else {
987 |             sb.append(c);
988 |           }
989 |           break;
990 |         default:
991 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
992 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
993 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
994 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
995 |               sb.append(c);
996 |             } else {
997 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
998 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
999 |             }
1000 |           } else {
1001 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
1002 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1003 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1004 |             if (c > 0x1f && c <= 0x7f) {
1005 |               sb.append(c);
1006 |             } else {
1007 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
1008 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1009 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
1010 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1011 |             }
1012 |           }
1013 |       }
1014 |     }
1015 |     sb.append(quote);
1016 |     return sb.toString();
1017 |   }

===== M0269 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 755-797
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
755 |   @Override
756 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
757 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
758 |       return true;
759 |     }
760 | 
761 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
762 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
763 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
764 |         return true;
765 |       }
766 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
767 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
768 |         return false;
769 |       }
770 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
771 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
772 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
773 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
774 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
775 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
776 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
777 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
778 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
779 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
780 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
781 | 
782 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
783 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
784 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
785 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
786 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
787 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
788 | 
789 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
790 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
791 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
792 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
793 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
794 |     }
795 | 
796 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
797 |   }

===== M0270 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0282 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1404-1427
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1404 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1405 |     boolean eq = false;
1406 | 
1407 |     if (type == node2.getType() && getChildCount() == node2.getChildCount()
1408 |         && getNodeClass(this) == getNodeClass(node2)) {
1409 |       eq = this.isEquivalentTo(node2);
1410 |     }
1411 | 
1412 |     if (!eq) {
1413 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1414 |     }
1415 | 
1416 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1417 |     Node n, n2;
1418 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1419 |          res == null && n != null;
1420 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1421 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1422 |       if (res != null) {
1423 |         return res;
1424 |       }
1425 |     }
1426 |     return res;
1427 |   }

   * Initializes the instance state needed for a compile job if the sources

295 |   public void initModules(
296 |       List<JSSourceFile> externs, List<JSModule> modules,
297 |       CompilerOptions options) {

310 |         this.moduleGraph = new JSModuleGraph(modules);

318 |     } else {

320 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateFunctions(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 234-256
Documentation:
/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(n);
236 | 
237 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
238 |       // which are var_args.
239 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
240 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
241 |            arg != null;
242 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
243 |         String argName = arg.getString();
244 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
245 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
246 | 
247 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
248 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
249 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
250 |         }
251 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
252 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
253 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
254 |         }
255 |       }
256 |     }

===== M0318 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 447-456
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
447 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
448 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
449 |          current != null;
450 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
451 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
452 |         return true;
453 |       }
454 |     }
455 |     return false;
456 |   }

===== M0319 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler,String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 84-106
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
85 |       String sourceStr) {
86 |     try {
87 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
90 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
91 |           logger_);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
106 |   }

===== M0320 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 779-793
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
779 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
780 |     switch (n.getType()) {
781 |       case Token.DO:
782 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
783 |       case Token.FOR:
784 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
785 |           return n;
786 |         }
787 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
788 |       case Token.LABEL:
789 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
790 |       default:
791 |         return n;
792 |     }
793 |   }

===== M0327 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneTree()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1865-1879
Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1865 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1866 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1867 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1868 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
1869 |       n2clone.parent = result;
1870 |       if (result.last != null) {
1871 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
1872 |       }
1873 |       if (result.first == null) {
1874 |         result.first = n2clone;
1875 |       }
1876 |       result.last = n2clone;
1877 |     }
1878 |     return result;
1879 |   }

===== M0328 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.redeclareVarsInsideBranch(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1791-1807
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a node tree, finds all the VAR declarations in that tree that are
   * not in an inner scope. Then adds a new VAR node at the top of the current
   * scope that redeclares them, if necessary.
   */
Method code:
1791 |   static void redeclareVarsInsideBranch(Node branch) {
1792 |     Collection<Node> vars = getVarsDeclaredInBranch(branch);
1793 |     if (vars.isEmpty()) {
1794 |       return;
1795 |     }
1796 | 
1797 |     Node parent = getAddingRoot(branch);
1798 |     for (Node nameNode : vars) {
1799 |       Node var = new Node(
1800 |           Token.VAR,
1801 |           Node.newString(Token.NAME, nameNode.getString())
1802 |               .copyInformationFrom(nameNode))
1803 |           .copyInformationFrom(nameNode);
1804 |       copyNameAnnotations(nameNode, var.getFirstChild());
1805 |       parent.addChildToFront(var);
1806 |     }
1807 |   }

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0344 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 82-112
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
83 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
84 |       sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
85 | 
86 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
87 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
88 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
89 | 
90 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
91 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
92 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
93 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
94 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
95 |       }
96 | 
97 |       // Args: Declare function variables
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
99 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
100 |            a = a.getNext()) {
101 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
102 |         declareVar(a);
103 |       }
104 | 
105 |       // Body
106 |       scanVars(body, n);
107 |     } else {
108 |       // It's the global block
109 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
110 |       scanVars(n, null);
111 |     }
112 |   }

===== M0345 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SourceFile.getLine(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SourceFile.java
Original range: 147-182
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the source line for the indicated line number.
   *
   * @param lineNumber the line number, 1 being the first line of the file.
   * @return The line indicated. Does not include the newline at the end
   *     of the file. Returns {@code null} if it does not exist,
   *     or if there was an IO exception.
   */
Method code:
147 |   public String getLine(int lineNumber) {
148 |     String js = "";
149 |     try {
150 |       // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, this is optimized for few warnings.
151 |       // This is probably the right trade-off, but will be slow if there
152 |       // are lots of warnings in one file.
153 |       js = getCode();
154 |     } catch (IOException e) {
155 |       return null;
156 |     }
157 | 
158 |     int pos = 0;
159 |     int startLine = 1;
160 | 
161 |     // If we've saved a previous offset and it's for a line less than the
162 |     // one we're searching for, then start at that point.
163 |     if (lineNumber >= lastLine) {
164 |       pos = lastOffset;
165 |       startLine = lastLine;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     for (int n = startLine; n < lineNumber; n++) {
169 |       int nextpos = js.indexOf('\n', pos);
170 |       if (nextpos == -1) {
171 |         return null;
172 |       }
173 |       pos = nextpos + 1;
174 |     }
175 | 
176 |     // Remember this offset for the next search we do.
177 |     lastOffset = pos;
178 |     lastLine = lineNumber;
179 | 
180 |     return (js.indexOf('\n', pos) == -1) ? null :
181 |         js.substring(pos, js.indexOf('\n', pos));
182 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
65 |   private String replacePlaceHolders(String s) {

===== M0360 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 775-789
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
775 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
776 |     if (item == null) {
777 |       return null;
778 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
779 |       return item.next;
780 |     } else {
781 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.next, propType);
782 |       if (result != item.next) {
783 |         return new PropListItem(
784 |             item.type, item.intValue, item.objectValue, result);
785 |       } else {
786 |         return item;
787 |       }
788 |     }
789 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processInfixExpression(InfixExpression)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java

Documentation:

Method code:
566 |     @Override
567 |     Node processInfixExpression(InfixExpression exprNode) {
568 |       Node n =  newNode(
569 |           transformTokenType(exprNode.getType()),
570 |           transform(exprNode.getLeft()),
571 |           transform(exprNode.getRight()));
572 |       // Set the line number here so we can fine-tune it in ways transform
573 |       // doesn't do.
574 |       n.setLineno(exprNode.getLineno());
575 |       // Position in new ASTNode is to start of expression, but old-fashioned
576 |       // line numbers from Node reference the operator token.  Add the offset
577 |       // to the operator to get the correct character number.
578 |       n.setCharno(position2charno(exprNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
579 |           exprNode.getOperatorPosition()));
580 |       return n;
581 |     }

===== M0382 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processSwitchCase(SwitchCase)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 717-737
Documentation:

Method code:
717 |     @Override
718 |     Node processSwitchCase(SwitchCase caseNode) {
719 |       Node node;
720 |       if (caseNode.isDefault()) {
721 |         node = newNode(Token.DEFAULT);
722 |       } else {
723 |         AstNode expr = caseNode.getExpression();
724 |         node = newNode(Token.CASE, transform(expr));
725 |       }
726 |       Node block = newNode(Token.BLOCK);
727 |       block.putBooleanProp(Node.SYNTHETIC_BLOCK_PROP, true);
728 |       block.setLineno(caseNode.getLineno());
729 |       block.setCharno(position2charno(caseNode.getAbsolutePosition()));
730 |       if (caseNode.getStatements() != null) {
731 |         for (AstNode child : caseNode.getStatements()) {
732 |           block.addChildToBack(transform(child));
733 |         }
734 |       }
735 |       node.addChildToBack(block);
736 |       return node;
737 |     }

===== M0387 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0392 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 622-649
Documentation:

Method code:
622 |   private void handleReturn(Node node) {
623 |     Node lastJump = null;
624 |     for (Iterator<Node> iter = exceptionHandler.iterator(); iter.hasNext();) {
625 |       Node curHandler = iter.next();
626 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunction(curHandler)) {
627 |         break;
628 |       }
629 |       if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(curHandler)) {
630 |         if (lastJump == null) {
631 |           createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, curHandler.getLastChild());
632 |         } else {
633 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump,
634 |               computeFallThrough(curHandler.getLastChild()));
635 |         }
636 |         lastJump = curHandler;
637 |       }
638 |     }
639 | 
640 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
641 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
642 |     }
643 | 
644 |     if (lastJump == null) {
645 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, null);
646 |     } else {
647 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, null);
648 |     }
649 |   }

Method code:
214 |   private void throwUnexpectedException(Exception unexpectedException) {
215 |     // If there's an unexpected exception, try to get the
216 |     // line number of the code that caused it.
217 |     String message = unexpectedException.getMessage();
218 | 
219 |     // TODO(user): It is possible to get more information if curNode or
220 |     // its parent is missing. We still have the scope stack in which it is still
221 |     // very useful to find out at least which function caused the exception.
222 |     if (!sourceName.isEmpty()) {

225 |           formatNodeContext("Node", curNode) +
226 |           (curNode == null ?
227 |               "" :

229 |     }
230 |     compiler.throwInternalError(message, unexpectedException);
231 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1146-1154
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1146 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1147 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1148 |       return -1;
1149 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1150 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1151 |     } else {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1153 |     }
1154 |   }

===== M0414 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleBreak(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 549-586
Documentation:

Method code:
549 |   private void handleBreak(Node node) {
550 |     String label = null;
551 |     // See if it is a break with label.
552 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
553 |       label = node.getFirstChild().getString();
554 |     }
555 |     Node cur;
556 |     Node lastJump;
557 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
558 |     /*
559 |      * Continuously look up the ancestor tree for the BREAK target or the target
560 |      * with the corresponding label and connect to it. If along the path we
561 |      * discover a FINALLY, we will connect the BREAK to that FINALLY. From then
562 |      * on, we will just record the control flow changes in the finallyMap. This
563 |      * is due to the fact that we need to connect any node that leaves its own
564 |      * FINALLY block to the outer FINALLY or the BREAK's target but those nodes
565 |      * are not known yet due to the way we traverse the nodes.
566 |      */
567 |     for (cur = node, lastJump = node;
568 |         !isBreakTarget(cur, label);
569 |         cur = parent, parent = parent.getParent()) {
570 |       if (cur.getType() == Token.TRY && NodeUtil.hasFinally(cur)) {
571 |         if (lastJump == node) {
572 |           createEdge(lastJump, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(
573 |               cur.getLastChild()));
574 |         } else {
575 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump, computeFallThrough(cur.getLastChild()));
576 |         }
577 |         lastJump = cur;
578 |       }
579 |       Preconditions.checkState(parent != null, "Cannot find break target.");
580 |     }
581 |     if (lastJump == node) {
582 |       createEdge(lastJump, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(cur, this));
583 |     } else {
584 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, computeFollowNode(cur, this));
585 |     }
586 |   }

===== M0436 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 420-446
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
420 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
421 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
422 | 
423 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
424 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
425 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
426 |       return 0;
427 |     }
428 | 
429 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
430 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
431 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
432 |       // a new statistic may have been added
433 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
434 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
435 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
436 |       }
437 |     }
438 | 
439 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
440 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
441 |       return 0;
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
445 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
446 |   }

===== M0440 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.UnreachableCodeElimination.tryRemoveUnconditionalBranching(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/UnreachableCodeElimination.java
Original range: 130-192
Documentation:
/**
   * Tries to remove n if an unconditional branch node (break, continue or
   * return) if the target of n is the same as the the follow of n. That is, if
   * we remove n, the control flow remains the same. Also if n targets to
   * another unconditional branch, this function will recursively try to remove
   * the target branch as well. The reason why we want to cascade this removal
   * is because we only run this pass once. If we have code such as
   *
   * break -> break -> break
   *
   * where all 3 break's are useless. The order of removal matters. When we
   * first look at the first break, we see that it branches to the 2nd break.
   * However, if we remove the last break, the 2nd break becomes useless and
   * finally the first break becomes useless as well.
   *
   * @return The target of this jump. If the target is also useless jump,
   *     the target of that useless jump recursively.
   */
Method code:
130 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
131 |   private Node tryRemoveUnconditionalBranching(Node n) {
132 |     /*
133 |      * For each of the unconditional branching control flow node, check to see
134 |      * if the ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode of that node is same as
135 |      * the branching target. If it is, the branch node is safe to be removed.
136 |      *
137 |      * This is not as clever as MinimizeExitPoints because it doesn't do any
138 |      * if-else conversion but it handles more complicated switch statements
139 |      * much nicer.
140 |      */
141 | 
142 |     // If n is null the target is the end of the function, nothing to do.
143 |     if (n == null) {
144 |        return n;
145 |     }
146 | 
147 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> gNode = curCfg.getDirectedGraphNode(n);
148 | 
149 |     if (gNode == null) {
150 |       return n;
151 |     }
152 | 
153 |     if (n.getParent() == null) {
154 |       List<DiGraphEdge<Node,Branch>> outEdges = gNode.getOutEdges();
155 |       if (outEdges.size() == 1) {
156 |         return tryRemoveUnconditionalBranching(outEdges.get(0).getDestination().getValue());
157 |       }
158 |     }
159 |     switch (n.getType()) {
160 |       case Token.BLOCK:
161 |         if (n.hasChildren()) {
162 |           Node first = n.getFirstChild();
163 |           return tryRemoveUnconditionalBranching(first);
164 |         } else {
165 |           return tryRemoveUnconditionalBranching(ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(n));
166 |         }
167 |       case Token.RETURN:
168 |         if (n.hasChildren()) {
169 |           break;
170 |         }
171 |       case Token.BREAK:
172 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
173 | 
174 |         // We are looking for a control flow changing statement that always
175 |         // branches to the same node. If removing it the control flow still
176 |         // branches to that same node. It is safe to remove it.
177 |         List<DiGraphEdge<Node,Branch>> outEdges = gNode.getOutEdges();
178 |         if (outEdges.size() == 1 &&
179 |             // If there is a next node, there is no chance this jump is useless.
180 |             (n.getNext() == null || n.getNext().getType() == Token.FUNCTION)) {
181 | 
182 |           Preconditions.checkState(outEdges.get(0).getValue() == Branch.UNCOND);
183 |           Node fallThrough = tryRemoveUnconditionalBranching(computeFollowing(n));
184 |           Node nextCfgNode = outEdges.get(0).getDestination().getValue();
185 |           if (nextCfgNode == fallThrough) {
186 |             removeDeadExprStatementSafely(n);
187 |             return fallThrough;
188 |           }
189 |         }
190 |     }
191 |     return n;
192 |   }

===== M0451 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 362-377
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
362 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
363 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
364 |       Set<String> directives = null;
365 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
366 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
367 |         if (directives == null) {
368 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
369 |         } else {
370 |           directives.add(directive);
371 |         }
372 |       }
373 | 
374 |       if (directives != null) {
375 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
376 |       }
377 |     }

185 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {

===== M0469 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleSwitch(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 432-447
Documentation:

Method code:
432 |   private void handleSwitch(Node node) {
433 |     // Transfer to the first non-DEFAULT CASE. if there are none, transfer
434 |     // to the DEFAULT or the EMPTY node.
435 |     Node next = getNextSiblingOfType(
436 |         node.getFirstChild().getNext(), Token.CASE, Token.EMPTY);
437 |     if (next != null) { // Has at least one CASE or EMPTY
438 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, next);
439 |     } else { // Has no CASE but possibly a DEFAULT
440 |       if (node.getFirstChild().getNext() != null) {
441 |         createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, node.getFirstChild().getNext());
442 |       } else { // No CASE, no DEFAULT
443 |         createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
444 |       }
445 |     }
446 |     connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
447 |   }

===== M0470 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleIf(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 357-370
Documentation:

Method code:
357 |   private void handleIf(Node node) {
358 |     Node thenBlock = node.getFirstChild().getNext();
359 |     Node elseBlock = thenBlock.getNext();
360 |     createEdge(node, Branch.ON_TRUE, computeFallThrough(thenBlock));
361 | 
362 |     if (elseBlock == null) {
363 |       createEdge(node, Branch.ON_FALSE,
364 |           computeFollowNode(node, this)); // not taken branch
365 |     } else {
366 |       createEdge(node, Branch.ON_FALSE, computeFallThrough(elseBlock));
367 |     }
368 |     connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(
369 |         node, NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(node));
370 |   }

===== M0478 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1040-1177
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1040 |   Node parseInputs() {
1041 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1042 | 
1043 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1044 |     // individual file parse trees.
1045 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1046 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1047 |     }
1048 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1049 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1050 |     }
1051 | 
1052 |     // Parse main js sources.
1053 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1054 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1055 | 
1056 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1057 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1058 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1059 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1060 |     }
1061 | 
1062 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1063 | 
1064 |     try {
1065 |       // Parse externs sources.
1066 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1067 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1068 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1069 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1070 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1071 |           return null;
1072 |         }
1073 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1074 |       }
1075 | 
1076 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1077 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1078 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1079 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1080 | 
1081 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1082 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1083 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1084 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1085 |           }
1086 |         }
1087 | 
1088 |         try {
1089 |           inputs =
1090 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1091 |               .manageDependencies(
1092 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1093 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1094 |           report(JSError.make(
1095 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1096 |           return null;
1097 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1098 |           report(JSError.make(
1099 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1100 |           return null;
1101 |         }
1102 |       }
1103 | 
1104 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1105 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1106 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1107 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1108 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1109 |           return null;
1110 |         }
1111 | 
1112 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1113 |         if (n == null) {
1114 |           continue;
1115 |         }
1116 | 
1117 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1118 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1119 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1120 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1121 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1122 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1123 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1124 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1125 | 
1126 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1127 | 
1128 |             externs.add(input);
1129 |             staleInputs = true;
1130 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1131 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1132 |             staleInputs = true;
1133 |           }
1134 |         }
1135 |       }
1136 | 
1137 |       if (staleInputs) {
1138 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1139 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1140 |       }
1141 | 
1142 |       // Build the AST.
1143 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1144 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1145 |         if (n == null) {
1146 |           continue;
1147 |         }
1148 | 
1149 |         if (devMode) {
1150 |           runSanityCheck();
1151 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1152 |             return null;
1153 |           }
1154 |         }
1155 | 
1156 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1157 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1158 | 
1159 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1160 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1161 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1162 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1163 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1164 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1165 |         }
1166 | 
1167 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1168 |       }
1169 | 
1170 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1171 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1172 | 
1173 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1174 |     } finally {
1175 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1176 |     }
1177 |   }

     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line

456 |     @Override
457 |     void maybeCutLine() {

===== M0503 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleCase(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 449-469
Documentation:

Method code:
449 |   private void handleCase(Node node) {
450 |     // Case is a bit tricky....First it goes into the body if condition is true.
451 |     createEdge(node, Branch.ON_TRUE,
452 |         node.getFirstChild().getNext());
453 |     // Look for the next CASE, skipping over DEFAULT.
454 |     Node next = getNextSiblingOfType(node.getNext(), Token.CASE);
455 |     if (next != null) { // Found a CASE
456 |       Preconditions.checkState(next.getType() == Token.CASE);
457 |       createEdge(node, Branch.ON_FALSE, next);
458 |     } else { // No more CASE found, go back and search for a DEFAULT.
459 |       Node parent = node.getParent();
460 |       Node deflt = getNextSiblingOfType(
461 |         parent.getFirstChild().getNext(), Token.DEFAULT);
462 |       if (deflt != null) { // Has a DEFAULT
463 |         createEdge(node, Branch.ON_FALSE, deflt);
464 |       } else { // No DEFAULT found, go to the follow of the SWITCH.
465 |         createEdge(node, Branch.ON_FALSE, computeFollowNode(node, this));
466 |       }
467 |     }
468 |     connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
469 |   }

===== M0505 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 157-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
157 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
158 |     if (isAllType) {
159 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
160 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
161 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
162 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
163 |       } else {
164 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
165 |       }
166 |     } else {
167 |       int size = alternates.size();
168 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
169 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
170 |       } else if (size > 1) {
171 |         return null;
172 |       } else if (size == 1) {
173 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
174 |       } else {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0510 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

===== M0514 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 221-257
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
221 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
222 |     this.options = options;
223 |     if (errorManager == null) {
224 |       if (outStream == null) {
225 |         setErrorManager(
226 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
227 |       } else {
228 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
229 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
230 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
231 |         setErrorManager(printer);
232 |       }
233 |     }
234 | 
235 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
236 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
237 |     guards.add(
238 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
239 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
240 |     WarningsGuard warningsGuard = options.getWarningsGuard();
241 |     if (warningsGuard != null) {
242 |       guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
243 |     }
244 | 
245 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
246 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
247 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
248 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
249 |     // error off.
250 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
251 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
252 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
253 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
254 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
255 |     }
256 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
257 |   }

===== M0520 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()

Original range: 430-450

Method code:
430 |     @Override
431 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
432 |       if (lineBreak) {
433 |         if (sawFunction) {
434 |           startNewLine();
435 |           sawFunction = false;
436 |         }
437 |       }
438 | 
439 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
440 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
441 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
442 |       int len = code.length();
443 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
444 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
445 |         if (ch == ';') {
446 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
447 |         }
448 |       }
449 |       maybeCutLine();
450 |     }

===== M0521 =====

68 |   private void addGuards(Iterable<WarningsGuard> guards) {

Method code:
158 |   private Node transformBlock(AstNode node) {
159 |     Node irNode = transform(node);
160 |     if (irNode.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
161 |       if (irNode.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
162 |         irNode.setType(Token.BLOCK);
163 |         irNode.setWasEmptyNode(true);
164 |       } else {
165 |         Node newBlock = newNode(Token.BLOCK, irNode);

169 |       }
170 |     }
171 |     return irNode;

/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
286 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
287 |     if (prototype == null) {
288 |       return false;
289 |     }
290 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
291 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
292 |       return false;

296 |

301 |       }
302 |     }
303 |     return true;
304 |   }
