===== M0549 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 599-611
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
599 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
600 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
601 |     if (typeSet == null) {
602 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
603 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
607 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
608 | 
609 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
610 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
611 |   }

===== M0556 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 503-553
Documentation:

Method code:
503 |    @Override
504 |   Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
505 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
506 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
507 |       if (name == null) {
508 |         name = new Name();
509 |         name.setIdentifier("");
510 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
511 |       }
512 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
513 |       Node newName = transform(name);
514 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
515 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
516 |         // declaration.
517 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
518 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
519 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
520 |         // same line as the function keyword.
521 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
522 |             functionNode.getLp();
523 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
524 |       }
525 | 
526 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
527 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
528 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
529 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
530 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
531 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
532 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
533 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
534 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
535 |       if (fnName != null) {
536 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
537 |       } else {
538 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
539 |       }
540 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
541 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
542 | 
543 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
544 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
545 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
546 |       }
547 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
548 | 
549 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
550 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
551 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
552 |      return node;
553 |     }

===== M0557 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.Config.buildAnnotationNames(Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/Config.java
Original range: 75-88
Documentation:
/**
   * Create the annotation names from the user-specified
   * annotation whitelist.
   */
Method code:
75 |   private static Map<String, Annotation> buildAnnotationNames(
76 |       Set<String> annotationWhitelist) {
77 |     ImmutableMap.Builder<String, Annotation> annotationBuilder =
78 |         ImmutableMap.builder();
79 |     annotationBuilder.putAll(Annotation.recognizedAnnotations);
80 |     for (String unrecognizedAnnotation : annotationWhitelist) {
81 |       if (!Annotation.recognizedAnnotations.containsKey(
82 |               unrecognizedAnnotation)) {
83 |         annotationBuilder.put(
84 |             unrecognizedAnnotation, Annotation.NOT_IMPLEMENTED);
85 |       }
86 |     }
87 |     return annotationBuilder.build();
88 |   }
